Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the math_analysis object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 989. The moduli space has dimension at most 660 with 3 connected components.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the math_analysis space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 989 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 660. QED.
